# Full-Container Priority Leads For Garlic

Prepared for Unyra Group LLC on 2026-04-19.

## Objective

Maintain the current shortlist of garlic buyers with the highest probability of converting into full-container sales.

## Ranking logic

This shortlist favors leads that combine:

- large or strategically important import markets;
- importer, distributor, wholesaler, processor, or foodservice fit;
- alignment to one of Unyra's three offers: fresh whole garlic, peeled refrigerated garlic, or frozen/IQF garlic;
- official-site contactability;
- public buyer or shipment evidence where available.

Pure small-format retail signals are deprioritized unless the company also has importer, distributor, or wholesale evidence.

Treat these as shipment-signal leads until paid customs data confirms beneficial garlic buyer ownership and decision-maker contacts. |

This cluster is best for processed, paste, minced, frozen/IQF or ingredient garlic validation rather than first-pass fresh whole FCL. |

IDESCO, ACOR, BTG and Continental add public food import-distribution and FMCG route-to-market evidence. |

MOLIGOO/Goua Group adds long-standing import of milk, juice, biscuits, sweets, tomato paste and food/household products from multiple international origins. |
| W391 | Wafa Holding / Noor Holding / Weddady Group / General Mauritania Food | Mauritania | Agroindustry, processor and cold-chain validation | Wafa validates group-level import-export/distribution plus large flour, bakery, fish-processing and agro-industrial capacity. Noor validates import-export, distribution, commercial representation, ILA Distribution, seafood processing and preservation. Weddady adds public agroindustry, wholesale-trader and food-product adjacency. General Mauritania Food adds agrofood production, transformation and packaging. This cluster is stronger for processed, foodservice, ingredient, peeled/minced or distributor-referral garlic than confirmed fresh whole FCL ownership. |
| W392 | MALAK Distribution / General Trade RIM / BICOG / CREP-Consulting | Mauritania | Fresh-produce and commodity-trade validation | MALAK is the clearest public fresh-produce lead because Go Africa Online identifies fruit, vegetable and general food-product distribution. CREP-Consulting adds supplier representation for basic food commodities such as sugar, rice, cooking oil, milk powder and tomato concentrate. These names should be screened in paid customs data for HS 070320 before FCL quoting. |

This cluster is downstream or cross-border but useful for format-specific demand and distributor referrals. |

| W655 | Valkoliekki / Kespro / Wihuri Metro-tukku / Elatukku / Valio Aimo / Meira Nova / Satotukku | Finland | Direct garlic processor-importer plus national peeled, frozen and fresh produce wholesale validation | Finland is now ranked around contact-ready official garlic-format evidence rather than small processors or retail SKU demand. Valkoliekki is the first Finland FCL validation target because official pages say it imports Spanish garlic itself, machine-peels cloves, supplies fresh, frozen and whole peeled garlic packs to Finland's largest wholesalers and food-industry buyers, and refines around 200,000 kg of garlic annually. Kespro, Wihuri Metro-tukku and Cemet/Elatukku are the strongest peeled/frozen foodservice channels, with public peeled garlic, frozen chopped garlic and frozen peeled garlic product pages. Valio Aimo and Meira Nova add national HoReCa delivery-wholesale scale and public procurement/customer contacts. Satotukku adds the strongest fresh whole-garlic produce-wholesale challenger, supported by official imported-vegetable/cold-chain scale and S-kaupat product data linking Satotukku to Egyptian fresh garlic. Garlica, Apetit, Sevan, Jaakkolan, Fresh Servant, Herkkumaa and Fine Foods remain secondary processor/ingredient routes; use paid customs to separate HS 070320 fresh whole ownership from peeled, frozen, processed and retail-private-label flows before quoting Finland containers. |
| W656 | Churrasqueria Veneza / Las Leñas / Casa Marco / Grãos / Global Trading / SCI / IESP | Paraguay | Fresh whole-garlic shipment ownership plus processed-garlic and foodservice validation | Paraguay is now ranked around public HS 070320 fresh-garlic ownership before processed-garlic or broad distributor scale. Churrasqueria Veneza is the first paid-customs FCL target because Trademo lists 141 import shipments and primarily garlic/fresh garlic with China as the top source, while Volza separately shows 143 HSN 0703 imports and public 27,000 kg and 54,000 kg fresh/refrigerated garlic/allium entries in June 2025. Las Leñas is the strongest contact-ready official route because its site says it leads Chinese garlic import and distribution in Paraguay, sells wholesale and retail from Mercado de Abasto, serves companies of multiple sizes and publishes direct phone/email. Casa Marco adds public 26,500 kg and 27,000 kg fresh-garlic/allium entries and ImportGenius shipment depth but needs official-contact validation. Grãos, Global Trading, Brigo, SCI, IESP, ETG and COPALSA validate processed garlic, minced garlic, granulated garlic and food-ingredient routes. Apolo, GP, Boxabo, Fresh & Frozen and 5ta. Gama add cold-chain, frozen/IQF and HoReCa channel scale. Use paid customs to confirm beneficial importer ownership and reachable contacts before quoting Paraguay containers. |
| W657 | Costa del Pacifico / Walmart Chile / Super Krak / Cebollas Chile / Polagro / PRINAL / Chinese Mark / Unified Foods | Chile | Fresh whole-garlic import ownership plus peeled, foodservice and ingredient validation | Chile is now ranked around public HS 07032090 fresh-garlic buyer evidence rather than domestic garlic-source/exporter evidence alone. Costa del Pacifico is the first paid-customs FCL target because Volza ranks it first among Chile garlic buyers with 32 shipments and HSN 07032090 exposure, with a separate profile showing HSN 0703 imports from China, Peru, Brazil and Ecuador. Walmart Chile and Super Krak add visible garlic-buyer shipment counts and large retail/wholesale or fruit-and-vegetable import-distribution scale. Cebollas Chile, Rogsfruit, La Rinconada and Nexco validate contact-ready Chilean garlic production/export channels. Polagro and Santiago Natural validate official fresh and peeled/wholesale garlic formats, while PRINAL, Chinese Mark, Unified Foods and Comercial AFL add garlic-oil, minced/picado, granulated/dehydrated and importer-distributor routes. Use paid customs to confirm beneficial HS 07032090 ownership and procurement contacts before quoting Chile containers. |

Pepe's Marketing is the first contact-ready FCL validation route because official pages validate bulk and fresh-produce garlic, 40+ years importing/exporting and distributing locally and regionally, chilled warehousing, 4,000-pallet expanded storage and regular container-load shipping from North America, South America, Europe and Asia. D Food and D&J add official dry-goods garlic and 20 lb garlic wholesale formats; ASM and ABL add public directory direct-garlic wholesaler leads that need phone/customs validation. CEJ adds 150+ annual import containers and national food/beverage wholesale coverage. Massy, HADCO, Malabar, Vasha, Persad's and Westco validate cold-chain, foodservice and produce/frozen distribution scale, while Pure Harvest and Chief cover garlic powder and seasoning formats. Use paid customs to separate fresh whole HS 070320 from granulated, garlic/onion, garlic powder, foodservice and dry-grocery flows before quoting Trinidad and Tobago containers. |

Guyimex remains the first contact-ready official route for fresh whole-garlic validation because its official site validates fresh-produce import, cold storage above 670 pallet spaces and service to wholesalers, restaurants, food processors, retailers, supermarkets, hotels and oil-and-gas buyers. Triple R is the first direct garlic shipment target from public dried-garlic container evidence, but lacks official-source strength and fresh-format proof. Massy Stores/Distribution, Alpha, AgroServices, Pan Global, Beepats and Faizal validate retail garlic demand, fresh-produce import/distribution and cold-chain/wholesale routes. Newrest, B.M. Enterprise, UnivEx, Rayo, VP Imports and Patsan add foodservice, fresh/frozen and broad import-distribution targets. Use paid customs to map HS 070320 beneficial owners before quoting Guyana containers. |

Exotic Fruit and Abiramy add public Volza buyer evidence for processed/dehydrated and fresh garlic, while Asia Engros and Norbygata add official frozen peeled garlic and garlic paste plus Trademo buyer-table support. Grönsaksmästarna remains the first contact-ready official fresh-garlic lead; Dole Nordic, Everfresh, Ewerman, Svensk FoodService, Hebe and FG Centralen are the first produce-infrastructure validation cluster. IND Traders is the strongest public processed-garlic customs signal from Volza HSN 21039090 and Trademo profile evidence, while Saigon Food, Matkompaniet and Hot Chilly/Taam add garlic-sauce and ethnic-grocery import evidence. GG Handel/Wernsing and Menigo validate frozen/IQF garlic formats, and Martin & Servera/Grönsakshallen/Outofhome validate peeled or foodservice garlic demand. Use paid customs before quoting to identify the beneficial fresh-garlic HS 070320 consignee layer. |

## Notes for sales execution

- Open with `fresh whole garlic` for ranks 1-3, 5, 7, 10, 11, 13, 16, 17, 18, 19, and 20 unless the buyer clearly prefers another format.
- Open with `peeled refrigerated garlic` for ranks 4, 6, 8, 9, 12, 14, 15, and the Ajo Conex processor path inside rank 16.
- Open with `frozen/IQF garlic` when the buyer already handles industrial, foodservice, or processed formats, especially ranks 6 and 14.
- Use [priority-buyers-garlic-2026.md](/Users/ninglobalservices/Documents/Unyra%20Group%20LLC/research/garlic/priority-buyers-garlic-2026.md) as the shipment-signal overlay before final outreach priority decisions.

- Expand coverage toward one high-conviction lead per country while preserving a tighter global FCL shortlist.
